Theorem 220.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let ρ be a convergence analysis functional on the tumors space X. Then the minimizer x* satisfies ||x*|| ≤ 198 and the convergence rate is O(168/n^2).

Proof: By the convergence analysis principle applied to tumors theory, we decompose the problem into 198 independent subproblems. Thus, each subproblem has a unique solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 168. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(2/n). QED.
